Company to report whether the trajectory should be terminated (e.g., single-TRP) or be retained (e.g., multi-TRP), if the UE trajectory hits the site boundary (the hexagonal grid) Other UE mobility model(s), e.g., EVM for NR ICBM, linear trajectory model with random and smooth direction change (i.e., TR38.843, Option-2), are not precluded (companies to report). \|<br>\| #30 Scheduling algorithm \| PF scheduler \|<br>\| #31 MCS \| Use NR MCS \|<br>\| #32 Traffic Model \| FTP model 1/3 with packet size 0.5 Mbytes (other value is not precluded) Other traffic models are not precluded (companies to report). Full buffer (Optional) \|<br>\| #33 CSI Feedback \| CSI periodicity: 10ms, 20ms (baseline), 40ms, 80ms, 160ms FFS: Other update for high-speed train \|<br>\| #34 Resource Utilization \| 30%, 50%, 70% (optional) \|<br>\| #35 Prediction parameter \| Company can report assumption for set-A and set-B, prediction window and any extension for intra-/inter-cell/multi-TRP measurement Set A: M*N M: {8, 16, 32, 64, 256,1024}. N: {1, 2, 3, 4}. Ratio of the size of Set-B to Set-A: {1, 1/2, 1/4, 1/8, 1/16, 1/32} For multi-TRP, Set-A and Set-B can contain beams across TRP(s). Other values for M, N and ratio are not precluded. Set A/Set B combinations: Alt1: Set B is different from Set A Alt2: Set B is a subset of Set A Alt3: Set B is the same as Set A Regarding temporal domain prediction, the following can be considered: Time gap between neighboring predicted time instances: {20ms, 40ms, 80ms, 120ms, 160ms, 200ms}. Predicted time instances: {1, 2, 4, 8}. Measured time instances: companies to report. Other values for the above are not precluded Other prediction mechanism is not precluded and up to companies’ report. \|<br>\| #36 UE number/cell \| Reuse the configuration(s) from 6GR air interface evaluation assumptions \|<br>\| #37 Layout/deployment \| Reuse the assumptions of uplink/downlink transmission schemes (10.5.2.2/3) 7 x 3, single layer, hex grid (baseline) 7 x 3 two layers, macro layer: hex grid; micro layer: locations of micro sites are reported by company (optional) 19 x 3, single layer, hex grid (optional) FFS: Other update for high-speed train Regarding high-speed train, reuse the assumptions of downlink/uplink transmission schemes (10.5.2.2/3) Regarding around 4 and 7 GHz, rural macro, 500 m or 1732 m ISD between RRH sites, two TRxPs per RRH site, one or more RRH sites connected to a BBU, with RRH site to railway track distance: 100 m Inter-track distance: 6 m Train length: 400 m Train width: 3.38 m Regarding around 30 GHz, rural macro, 500 m or 1732 m between BBU sites, one or more RRH sites connected to 1 BBU, one TRxP per RRH site, with RRH site to railway track distance: 5 m Inter-RRH site distance: [580 m, 580 m, 572 m] Inter-track distance: 6 m Train length: 400 m Train width: 3.38 m \|<br>\| #38 Inter-cell interference model \| Reuse the assumptions of DL-based CSI (10.5.3.1) Explicitly and realistically modelled \|<br>\| #39 Metric \| Top-K/1 beam prediction accuracy (with or without L1-RSRP margin), average/5%-ile UPT/throughput, SINR/RSRP, L1-RSRP/SINR difference between Top-1 predicted beam and genie-aided beam, RS overhead reduction, beam management latency, and/or number of model-parameter(s)/model computational complexity. Companies can report other performance metrics Companies report the assumption of transmission scheme, if UPT/throughput is reported. \| | [Chair notes RAN1#125_eom](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_125/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23125_eom.docx)<br>`[125-R20-6GR-Beam management]` |
| RAN1#125 | 10.5.2.4 Beam management for downlink and uplink | Beam Management | Agreement | Support the following table as the assumption of LLS for evaluation of 6GR beam management<br>- Revise the RAN1#124-bis agreement as follows:<br>\| Parameter \| Value \|<br>\| #1 Carrier Frequency \| 4GHz, 7GHz, 30GHz \|<br>\| #2 Duplex / Waveform \| TDD/CP-OFDM \|<br>\| #3 Subcarrier Spacing \| For 4GHz/7GHz: 30KHz; For 30GHz:120KHz \|<br>\| #4 Simulation Bandwidth \| Reuse the assumptions of PDSCH EVM (10.5.2.2) 20 MHz, 100 MHz Other bandwidths are not precluded (companies to report) \|<br>\| #5 Channel Model \| Reuse the assumptions of PDSCH EVM/CSI-others (10.5.2.2/10.5.3.3) CDL-A/B/C/D model in TR 38.901 The CDL table is translated so that the strongest cluster’s AoD and AoA occur at a random angle for both the antenna panels of TRP and UE in the local coordinate. The value of the random angle is selected to be uniformly distributed from +30 to -30 degree. The random value is chosen independently for both AoD and AoA. \|<br>\| #6 Delay Spread \| Reuse the assumptions of PDSCH EVM (10.5.2.2) 30 ns, 100 ns, 300 ns, 1000 ns (optional) \|<br>\| #7 NW Antenna Config \| Reuse the agreed configuration(s) from 6GR air interface evaluation assumptions For 30GHz, the following can be further considered: (M, N, P, Mg, Ng; Mp, Np) = (8, 16, 2, 1, 1; 1, 1), or (16, 32, 2, 1, 1; 1, 1)), (dH, dV) = (0.5, 0.5); \|<br>\| #8 Multi-TRP operation \| The maximum number of coordinated TRP(s) = {1 (baseline), 2, 3, 4}, and other larger value(s) are not precluded CDL channel model is generated per TRP independently; Backhaul: ideal (baseline) or non-ideal; Sync: ideal (baseline) or non-ideal; Companies explain the details, if any. \|<br>\| #9 TXRU mapping to antenna elements \| A single TXRU is mapped per panel per subarray per polarization. Others are not precluded (companies to report). \|<br>\| #10 Beam-forming scheme \| Companies explain the details, e.g., DFT-based or Eigen-based \|<br>\| #11 Procedure of beam sweeping \| Companies explain the details \|<br>\| #12 Criteria for beam selection \| To maximize RSRP (with/without a margin (baseline for margin: 3dB, companies to report)) as a baseline. Other criteria is not precluded and up to companies to report. \|<br>\| #13 UE Antenna Config \| 4GHz & 7GHz: Reuse the agreed configuration(s) from 6GR air interface evaluation assumptions; 30GHz: Reuse the agreed configuration(s) from 6GR air interface evaluation assumptions: For one-panel, (M, N, P, Mg, Ng; Mp, Np) = (4, 1, 2, 1, 1; 1, 1) is considered as a baseline For multi-panel, (M, N, P; Mp, Np) = (2, 2, 2; 1, 1) is considered as a baseline. \|<br>\| #14 BS array orientation \| Azimuth 0°, mechanic downtilt: 90° in GCS. \|<br>\| #15 UE array orientation \| ΩUT,a uniformly distributed on [0, 360] degree, ΩUT,b = 0°, ΩUT,g = 0° \|<br>\| #16 UE antenna element radiation pattern \| For 4 GHz/7GHz: Option-1: Omni-directional with 0dBi gain; Option-2: According to Table 7.3-2 in TR 38.901 (radiation power pattern for handheld UT) For 30 GHz: See Table A.2.1-8 in TR 38.802 \|<br>\| #17 UE Velocity \| 3km/h, 30km/h, 120km/h (Optional), 350km/h(Optional), 500km/h (Optional) \|<br>\| #18 MIMO Scheme \| SU-MIMO/MU-MIMO \|<br>\| #19 CW-to-layer mapping \| Reuse the assumptions of CSI EVM (10.5.3.1) \|<br>\| #20 Channel coding \| LDPC for DL/UL data channel, Polar for CSI \|<br>\| #21 SSB, CSI-RS configuration for BM \| NR SSB, CSI-RS for BM as benchmark, and it is up to company to report other configuration, e.g., periodicity. \|<br>\| #22 SRS configuration for BM \| NR SRS with usage set to BM as baseline, and it is up to company to report other configuration, e.g., periodicity \|<br>\| #23 Receiver Type \| MMSE-IRC as baseline; R-ML (companies to report) \|<br>\| #24 Channel Estimation \| Ideal or realistic \|<br>\| #25 MCS \| NR MCS (fixed, or AMC) \|<br>\| #26 Performance Metric(s) \| BLER and Spectrum Efficiency (SE) Companies can report other performance metrics \| | [Chair notes RAN1#125_eom](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_125/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23125_eom.docx)<br>`[125-R20-6GR-Beam management]` |
| RAN1#125 | 10.5.3.1 Aspects of downlink-based CSI acquisition | CSI Acquisition / Prediction | Agreement | For the study of non-AI/AI based CSI prediction in frequency/spatial domain at UE side, consider the following aspects<br>- Prediction input (UE side Input): reduced CSI-RS measurement<br>- Additional information to be used by UE side for CSI prediction, if applicable<br>  - Option 1: Long-term channel information derived from full-dimensional CSI-RS measurement<br>  - Option 2: Reconstruction information (indicated by BS) applied on the measured channel based on non-precoded CSI-RS,<br>  - Option 3: Reconstruction information based on precoded CSI-RS (indicated by BS), e.g., precoder applied on CSI-RS<br>  - Option 4: DMRS based CSI measurement<br>  - Other options or combination of above options are not precluded<br>  - Companies are encouraged to study whether there is performance impact caused by aging/temporary unavailability of the additional information<br>- Prediction output (UE side Output): Predicted CSI to be used for CSI report<br>  - Note: UE side Output does not necessarily equal the CSI report quantity<br>  - Companies to report the details of the predicted CSI for CSI report | [Chair notes RAN1#125_eom](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_125/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23125_eom.docx)<br>`[125-R20-6GR-Aspects of DL based CSI acquisition]` |
| RAN1#125 | 10.5.3.1 Aspects of downlink-based CSI acquisition | CSI Acquisition / Prediction | Agreement | For study of DMRS-based CSI, considering at least the following aspects:<br>- Potential use cases, including e.g., increased CSI accuracy including inter-layer imbalance information, CSI-RS overhead reduction, CSI acquisition for TDD.<br>- Benchmark:<br>  - CSI-RS based<br>    - P CSI-RS and/or AP CSI-RS based (precoded or non-precoded)<br>  - SRS based<br>- Company to report CSI-RS/SRS assumption for benchmark<br>- Company to report the assumptions for DMRS resources and how DMRS is used for CSI measurement, e.g., only for channel measurement or both channel and interference measurement.<br>- Companies to report the OLLA assumption details, if applicable<br>- KPI:<br>  - Throughput (average and edge UPT) vs total CSI feedback overhead (averaged over UEs, over simulation time (per time unit)), where the total CSI feedback overhead includes both DMRS based feedback, and other necessary CSI feedback (if applicable)<br>  - Note: DL RS overhead is included in throughput calculation.<br>- DMRS based report quantity:<br>  - at least CQI, companies report CQI calculation assumption<br>  - companies can report other quantities, e.g., PMI (e.g., refinement), RI, RSRP/SINR, DMRS port selection indication if assumed, including the detailed assumption<br>- UE speed assumption follows the general EVM section 10.1 and other EVM follows the assumption agreed in RAN1#124. | [Chair notes RAN1#125_eom](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_125/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23125_eom.docx)<br>`[125-R20-6GR-Aspects of DL based CSI acquisition]` |
| RAN1#125 | 10.5.3.1 Aspects of downlink-based CSI acquisition | CSI Acquisition / Prediction | Agreement | For the 6GR codebook design for CSI report, the following is considered:<br>- NR fixed DFT basis is used as a baseline for evaluation for W1 and Wf  at least for non-precoded CSI-RS<br>  - For cell-specific precoded CSI-RS, port-selection basis and/or NR DFT basis can be used as a baseline for evaluation<br>  - FFS on other basis, e.g., for near field, basis combined from DFT beams/port selection, and non-orthogonal DFT basis<br>- Low- resolution scheme:<br>  - Max RI=8 as a baseline for evaluation<br>  - Candidate structures include Type-I Scheme-A like (W1 W2), Type-I Scheme-B like (W1 W2), eType-II like L=1 (W1 W2 Wf) structures<br>    - For eType-II like L=1, layer-specific/layer-group-specific SD basis selection<br>    - Other candidate schemes are not precluded<br>  - FFS: details on whether/how to accommodate passive array with smaller number of ports<br>- High- resolution scheme:<br>  - Max RI=4 as a baseline for evaluation<br>  - eType-II L>1 (W1 W2 Wf) structure as a baseline for evaluation (including implicit and explicit feedback)<br>  - Note: Whether/how to use this for explicit channel feedback is not precluded.<br>- For both low- and high-res, CJT codebook with NTRP>1 TRPs/panels<br>  - TRP/panel specific SD basis selection, TRP/panel common FD basis selection (if applicable) as a baseline for evaluation<br>  - Study whether/how to support dynamic TRP/panel selection mechanism<br>  - Company reports the multi-TRP/panel assumption, e.g., co-located, non-co-located<br>  - Note: The term “TRP” and “panel” are used for discussion purposes<br>Note: Additional details are still to be studied, including enhancements for UPT-overhead trade-off and UE complexity | [Chair notes RAN1#125_eom](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_125/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23125_eom.docx)<br>`[125-R20-6GR-Aspects of DL based CSI acquisition]` |
| RAN1#125 | 10.5.3.1 Aspects of downlink-based CSI acquisition | CSI Acquisition / Prediction | Agreement | For the study of non-AI/AI based CSI prediction in frequency/spatial domain at NW side, consider the following aspects<br>- Prediction input:<br>  - Option 1-1: UE reported channel matrix derived from reduced dimension CSI-RS measurement<br>  - Option 1-2: UE reported coefficient matrix, which is based on the multiplication of a downloaded matrix and the channel matrix derived from reduced dimension CSI-RS measurement<br>  - Option 1-3: UE reported precoding matrix derived from reduced dimension CSI-RS measurement<br>  - Option 1-4: UE reported coefficient matrix, which is based on the multiplication of a downloaded matrix and the precoding matrix derived from reduced dimension CSI-RS measurement<br>  - Other options are not precluded<br>  - Note: Performance impact caused by quantization of the UE reported CSI is part of the study<br>- Additional information to be used by NW side for CSI prediction, if applicable<br>  - Option 2-1: Long-term channel information corresponding to full dimension CSI reported by UE<br>  - Option 2-2: Channel information corresponding to full dimension CSI derived from UL measurement<br>  - Option 2-3: Channel measurement based on DL DMRS reported by UE<br>  - For spatial domain, “dimension” is defined in terms of ports<br>  - For frequency domain, companies to report the assumed granularity to define “full dimension”<br>- Prediction output: Full-dimension CSI to be used by BS<br>  - Note: companies are encouraged to report the details of the full-dimension CSI<br>- Note: companies to report the assumption of rank and MCS determination in the simulation | [Chair notes RAN1#125_eom](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_125/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23125_eom.docx)<br>`[125-R20-6GR-Aspects of DL based CSI acquisition]` |
| RAN1#125 | 10.5.3.1 Aspects of downlink-based CSI acquisition | CSI Acquisition / Prediction | Agreement | For RE locations of CDM groups in 6GR CSI-RS pattern design, support Option 1-1: Localized CDM group similar to 5G NR, where one CDM group contains adjacent REs. | [Chair notes RAN1#125_eom](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_125/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23125_eom.docx)<br>`[125-R20-6GR-Aspects of DL based CSI acquisition]` |
| RAN1#125 | 10.5.3.1 Aspects of downlink-based CSI acquisition | CSI Acquisition / Prediction | Agreement | For OCC sequence used in a 6GR CSI-RS CDM group, study the following options<br>- Option 1: Walsh sequence<br>- Option 2: DFT sequence | [Chair notes RAN1#125_eom](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_125/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23125_eom.docx)<br>`[125-R20-6GR-Aspects of DL based CSI acquisition]` |
| RAN1#125 | 10.5.3.1 Aspects of downlink-based CSI acquisition | CSI Acquisition / Prediction | Agreement | For the study of 6GR CSI-RS pattern in single or multiple slots/RBs in time/frequency domain<br>- The number of slots for defining CSI-RS pattern is M1, and the number of RBs for defining CSI-RS pattern is M2. Study the following further aspects in this case<br>  - For RE location in the M1 slots and M2 RBs,<br>    - Option 1-1: Restrict that CSI-RS pattern occupies a same set of REs in each of the M1 slots and M2 RBs<br>    - Option 1-2: CSI-RS pattern can occupy same or different set of REs in each of the M1 slots and M2 RBs<br>      - The number of REs occupied by CSI-RS in the M1 slots and M2 RBs can be same or different<br>    - FFS whether the set of REs occupied by CSI-RS changes across occasions<br>  - For mapping order of CDM groups in the M1 slots and M2 RBs<br>    - Option 2-1: Mapping order of CDM groups in the M1 slots and M2 RBs is fixed across multiple occasions/symbols<br>    - Option 2-2: Mapping order of CDM groups in the M1 slots and M2 RBs is switched across multiple occasions/symbols<br>  - Whether both or which one from M1 and M2 can be larger than 1 is to be further studied<br>  - Study details for M1 and M2, including both the upper bound and detailed values<br>- Note: for RAN1 study purpose, whether the CSI-RS pattern mentioned above is from NW or UE perspective is a separate discussion | [Chair notes RAN1#125_eom](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_125/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23125_eom.docx)<br>`[125-R20-6GR-Aspects of DL based CSI acquisition]` |
| RAN1#125 | 10.5.3.1 Aspects of downlink-based CSI acquisition | CSI Acquisition / Prediction | Agreement | For CSI-RS sharing use case 1 (sharing a common set or subset of REs among CSI-RS with different number of ports) in 6GR, study the following aspects<br>- Level 1: Sharing granularity is CDM group level<br>- Level 2: Sharing granularity can be smaller than a CDM group | [Chair notes RAN1#125_eom](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_125/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23125_eom.docx)<br>`[125-R20-6GR-Aspects of DL based CSI acquisition]` |
| RAN1#125 | 10.5.3.1 Aspects of downlink-based CSI acquisition | CSI Acquisition / Prediction | Agreement | For CSI-RS sharing use case 2 (Sharing a common set or subset of REs among CSI-RS with different frequency densities), study whether/how to achieve frequency density common sequence mapping. | [Chair notes RAN1#125_eom](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_125/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23125_eom.docx)<br>`[125-R20-6GR-Aspects of DL based CSI acquisition]` |
| RAN1#125 | 10.5.3.1 Aspects of downlink-based CSI acquisition | CSI Acquisition / Prediction | Agreement | For CJT calibration report,<br>- Study the following three types of reporting quantities:<br>- Inter-TRP (xTRP) delay offset, analogous to Rel-19 cjtc-Dd<br>- Inter-TRP (xTRP) frequency offset, analogous to Rel-19 cjtc-F<br>- Inter-TRP (xTRP) DL/UL phase offset, analogous to Rel-19 cjtc-P<br>- Note: Additional details are still to be studied, including dynamic range and resolution, and their additional use cases (if any)<br>- Stand-alone reporting as a baseline for evaluation<br>- Study whether/how to support reporting CJTC delay offset report and codebook-based CSI (with NTRP>1)<br>- Note: when CSI-RS and DMRS have different delay pre-compensation assumption, the potential impact on DMRS based channel estimation can be discussed in 10.5.2.2 | [Chair notes RAN1#125_eom](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_125/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23125_eom.docx)<br>`[125-R20-6GR-Aspects of DL based CSI acquisition]` |
| RAN1#125 | 10.5.3.1 Aspects of downlink-based CSI acquisition | CSI Acquisition / Prediction | Agreement | For the study on channel property information (CPI), the evaluation at least considering the following:<br>Category of use cases:<br>- CSI configuration/report adaptation for overhead/complexity reduction, CSI feedback with more accuracy, and/or CSI prediction<br>- Reference signal configuration adaptation (e.g., CSI-RS for CSI, DMRS, SRS)<br>- DL/UL transmission scheduling and configuration (e.g., coordinated beamforming/scheduling for inter-TRP/cell/user interference suppression)<br>- Note: the above categories can be applicable to sTRP/mTRP.<br>Basic categories of channel properties<br>- Spatial-domain (SD)<br>- Frequency-domain (FD)<br>- Time-domain (TD)<br>- Any combination of the above domains<br>Measurement definition and/or reporting formats, for example:<br>- Correlation information (e.g., time-/frequency/spatial-domain correlation)<br>- Spectrum (e.g., channel statistics in a transform domain, basis indices and power)<br>- Channel covariance matrix<br>- Explicit channel matrix<br>- Explicit system parameters (e.g. codebook parameters, RS parameters)<br>- Other formats are not precluded.<br>Note: Strive for common report quantities for use cases, if applicable.<br>Note: The related testability issue is up to RAN 4.<br>Note: LS to RAN 4 is to be considered for RAN 4 testability issues, when mature. | [Chair notes RAN1#125_eom](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_125/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23125_eom.docx)<br>`[125-R20-6GR-Aspects of DL based CSI acquisition]` |
| RAN1#125 | 10.5.3.1 Aspects of downlink-based CSI acquisition | CSI Acquisition / Prediction | Agreement | For SLS evaluation for JSCM-based CSI compression,<br>- Considering the following options of the SSCCM below could be used for the SLS evaluation as a starting point:<br>  - Option 1: variable RE overhead {Throughput vs CSI feedback overhead (# of REs)}<br>    - Step 1: for SSCCM, fixed payload determination (per PC or per payload)<br>      - Different parameter combination or payload is simulated.<br>    - Step 2: CSI feedback overhead (# of REs) is averaged of scheduled UEs in SLS, and the UL resource of each UE is determined based on the UL SINR considering link adaption<br>    - Step 3(if applicable): check whether UL transmission is successfully decoded or not<br>      - Based on the PHY abstraction (flip coin) according to UL SINR for each UE<br>        - Y: Calculate UPT based on the current CSI<br>        - N: Calculate UPT based on the historical CSI<br>    - Step 4: gNB uses the received CSI feedback for DL transmission for UPT calculation across UEs.<br>  - Option 2: Fixed CSI feedback overhead (#RE) with per UE level PC/payload selection for SSCCM and per scheduling UE level uplink link adaptation for SSCCM<br>    - Step 1: Fixed CSI feedback overhead (# of REs) is used across all UEs.<br>      - Payload determination (parameter combination/payload) for each UE<br>        - Companies report how to determine the parameter commination/payload<br>      - for each UE, the payload size can be selected according to UL SINR<br>        - Companies report how to generate UL SINR, e.g., based on geometry only, or based on geometry and fast fading.<br>        - Companies report the target BLER and UL SINR definition (e.g., port domain or UL SINR after Rx combination)<br>    - Step 2 (if applicable): check whether UL transmission is successfully decoded or not<br>      - Based on the PHY abstraction (flip coin) according to UL SINR for each UE<br>        - Y: Calculate UPT based on the current CSI and update the historical CSI<br>        - N: Calculate UPT based on the historical CSI<br>  - Option 3: variable RE overhead with per scheduled UE level PC/payload selection and per scheduling UE level uplink link adaptation for SSCCM<br>    - Step 1:<br>      - Payload determination (parameter combination/payload) and link adaptation to determine the variable RE for each UE<br>        - Companies report how to determine the parameter combination/payload and link adaption<br>    - Step 2 (if applicable): check whether UL transmission is successfully decoded or not<br>      - Based on the PHY abstraction (flip coin) according to UL SINR for each UE<br>        - Y: Calculate UPT based on the current CSI and update the historical CSI<br>        - N: Calculate UPT based on the historical CSI<br>        - Note: Error free can be reported.<br>    - Step 3: gNB uses the received CSI feedback for DL transmission for UPT calculation across UEs<br>  - Other refinement to the above options are not precluded.<br>  - Other options can be reported.<br>- SSCCM baseline assumption for SLS is up to companies’ report<br>- For JSCM, CSI feedback overhead is selected for each scheduled UEs in SLS.<br>  - Companies report how to select the CSI overhead for each UE for other method.<br>- Companies report how to generate UL SINR, e.g., based on geometry only, or based on geometry and fast fading.<br>  - Companies report the uplink channel estimation error assumption.<br>- Companies report the target BLER (e.g., error free, or 1% or 10%) and UL SINR definition (e.g., port domain or UL SINR after Rx combination) for SSCCM in Step 2.<br>- Fixed max Tx power for uplink, companies report MPR assumption in the simulation. Companies report the UL power control assumption, e.g., P0 and alpha values.<br>  - PRB is used as power control adjustment granularity<br>- Companies report constellation assumption and corresponding PAPR (considering potential multiplexing with other UCI/UL-SCH).<br>- 1Tx or multiple Tx can be considered for UL. If more than 1 Tx is assumed, companies report the UL Tx/precoder assumptions.<br>- Companies report the PHY abstraction for uplink based on polar coding, if applicable.<br>- Companies report the handling of scalability of number of REs for JSCM-based CSI compression, if applicable, and whether to adopt UCI only on PUSCH or UCI with UL-SCH for benchmark and JSCM-based compression.<br>- Companies report the assumptions of JSCM, e.g., the projection-matrix-based compression (compress target CSI by multiplying with a projection matrix at UE) or encoder-based (e.g., transformer-based)<br>- Companies report the assumption on obtaining projection matrix/encoder for JSCM-based CSI compression<br>- Companies report the assumption on DL CSI-RS channel estimation error:<br>  - Error modelling in TR36.897 Table A.1-2 as a baseline if channel estimation error is modelled. | [Chair notes RAN1#125_eom](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_125/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23125_eom.docx)<br>`[125-R20-6GR-Aspects of DL based CSI acquisition]` |
| RAN1#125 | 10.5.5 Other physical layer signals, channels and procedure | 6G Study Item (6GR, 기타) | Agreement | For BS-to-BS CLI measurement in 6GR:<br>- RAN1 recommends to use CSI-RS and synchronization signal (e.g., 6GR SSB) to measure BS-to-BS CLI or Channel.<br>  - Further study other channel or RS used for measuring BS-to-BS CLI.<br>- Study UL resource muting mechanism for handling BS-to-BS in 6GR<br>  - The UL resource muting mechanism specified in 5G/NR is the baseline for study.<br>  - Study both transparent and non-transparent UL resource muting mechanisms,<br>  - Study the mechanism with flexible UL resource muting symbol position(s) and flexible disable/enable indication.<br>  - Study UL resource muting pattern.<br>- Study the information exchange between the BSs, at least including the configuration of the CSI-RS and synchronization signal, measurement results, the recommended beam index and non-recommended beam index, the TDD time/frequency resources configuration, the SBFD time/frequency resources configuration. | [Chair notes RAN1#125_eom](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_125/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23125_eom.docx)<br>`[125-R20-6GR-Other signals, channels, and procedures]` |
| RAN1#125 | 8.1 Maintenance on AI/ML for NR Air interface, NR MIMO Phase 5,  NES and NTN for NR and IoT | Rel-19 Maintenance (AI/ML, MIMO Ph5, NES, NTN 등) | Agreement | The following TP for TS 38.214 Clause 5.4 is endorsed.<br>The corresponding CR for Rel-19 TS 38.214 in R1-2605124 is endorsed.<br>Reason for change: CSI computation time for the CSI report of RS-PAI has not been discussed or defined yet in current specification. Furthermore, the current specification would cause the RS-PAI report to fall under the branch of the “ of table 5.4-2 otherwise”, which would mistakenly apply high latency CSI computation time  to the RS-PAI report.<br>Summary of change: Reuse CSI computation time of legacy beam management, i.e. () for model monitoring CSI report of RS-PAI.<br>Consequences if not approved: UE may be unable to determine the appropriate CSI computation time for RS-PAI report and could erroneously use the high latency CSI computation time  for the RS-PAI report.<br>\| 5.4 UE CSI computation time <omitted texts> Z, Z' and µ are defined as: and , where M is the number of updated CSI report(s) according to Clause 5.2.1.6, corresponds to the m-th updated CSI report and is defined as - of the table 5.4-1 if max{ µPDCCH, µCSI-RS, µUL} ≤ 3 and if the CSI is triggered without a PUSCH with either transport block or HARQ-ACK or both when L = 0 CPUs are occupied (according to Clause 5.2.1.6) and the CSI to be transmitted is a single CSI and corresponds to wideband frequency-granularity where the CSI corresponds to at most 4 CSI-RS ports in a single resource without CRI report and where CodebookType is set to 'typeI-SinglePanel' or where reportQuantity is set to 'cri-RI-CQI', or - of the table 5.4-2 if the CSI to be transmitted corresponds to wideband frequency-granularity where the CSI corresponds to at most 4 CSI-RS ports in a single resource without CRI report and where CodebookType is set to 'typeI-SinglePanel' or where reportQuantity is set to 'cri-RI-CQI', or - of the table 5.4-2 if the CSI to be transmitted corresponds to wideband frequency-granularity where the reportQuantity is set to 'ssb-Index-SINR', 'cri-SINR', 'ssb-Index-SINR- Index ', or 'cri-SINR- Index ', or - of the table 5.4-2 if reportQuantity is set to 'cri-RSRP', 'ssb-Index-RSRP', 'cri-RSRP- Index', 'L1-SRS-RSRP', 'L1-CLI-RSSI' or, 'ssb-Index-RSRP- Index ' or 'rs-pai-r19', is according to UE reported capability beamReportTiming and KBl is according to UE reported capability beamSwitchTiming as defined in [13, TS 38.306], or if the CSI report is configured with ltm-CSI-ReportConfig for L1-RSRP measurement, or if the CSI report is configured with eventType, or - , with of the table 5.4-2 if reportQuantity is set to 'p-cri-r19', 'p-cri-RSRP-r19', 'p-ssb-index-r19', or 'p-ssb-index-RSRP-r19', where and are according to UE reported capability as defined in [13, TS 38.306] and different values for and may be reported by UE capability based on whether nroftimeinstance-r19 is configured or not within the CSI report, or <omitted texts> \| | [Chair notes RAN1#125_eom](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_125/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23125_eom.docx)<br>[R1-2604995](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_125/Docs/R1-2604995.zip) |
| RAN1#125 | 8.2 Maintenance on others | Rel-19 Maintenance (AI/ML, MIMO Ph5, NES, NTN 등) | Agreement | Adopt the following TP to TS38.215 Section 5.1.1<br>\| Reason for change: \| CSI-RS-based intra-frequency L1-RSRP measurement and inter-frequency L1-RSRP measurement for neighbor cell have been supported for LTM, but only CSI-RS-based intra-frequency L1-RSRP measurement is captured in TS38.215. Besides, in the last meeting, CR on adding the inter-frequency scenario to applicable scenarios of SS-RSRP for R18 LTM was agreed. But the corresponding description in the R19 specification has not been revised. \|<br>\| Summary of change \| Capture CSI-RS-based and SSB-based inter-frequency L1-RSRP measurement for neighbor cell in Rel-19 LTM. \|<br>\| Consequences if not approved \| CSI-RS-based inter-frequency L1-RSRP measurement and SSB-based inter-frequency L1-RSRP measurement for neighbor cell in Rel-19 LTM are not supported for Rel-19 LTM. \|<br>\| Clauses affected \| 5.1.1 \|<br>---------------------------------------------Start of TP#3-2-------------------------------------------------------------<br>5.1.1	SS reference signal received power (SS-RSRP)<br>\| Definition \| SS reference signal received power (SS-RSRP) is defined as the linear average over the power contributions (in [W]) of the resource elements that carry secondary synchronization signals. The measurement time resource(s) for SS-RSRP are confined within SS/PBCH Block Measurement Time Configuration (SMTC) window duration, except when measurement is being performed by wake-up receiver (WUR). If SS-RSRP is used for L1-RSRP as configured by reporting configurations as defined in TS 38.214 [6], the measurement time resources(s) restriction by SMTC window duration is not applicable. For SS-RSRP determination demodulation reference signals for physical broadcast channel (PBCH) and, if indicated by higher layers, CSI reference signals in addition to secondary synchronization signals may be used. SS-RSRP using demodulation reference signal for PBCH or CSI reference signal shall be measured by linear averaging over the power contributions of the resource elements that carry corresponding reference signals taking into account power scaling for the reference signals as defined in TS 38.213 [5]. If SS-RSRP is not used for L1-RSRP, the additional use of CSI reference signals for SS-RSRP determination is not applicable. SS-RSRP shall be measured only among the reference signals corresponding to SS/PBCH blocks with the same SS/PBCH block index and the same physical-layer cell identity. If SS-RSRP is not used for L1-RSRP and higher-layers indicate certain SS/PBCH blocks for performing SS-RSRP measurements, then SS-RSRP is measured only from the indicated set of SS/PBCH block(s). For frequency range 1, the reference point for the SS-RSRP shall be the antenna connector of the UE. For frequency range 2, SS-RSRP shall be measured based on the combined signal from antenna elements corresponding to a given receiver branch. For frequency range 1 and 2, if receiver diversity is in use by the UE, the reported SS-RSRP value shall not be lower than the corresponding SS-RSRP of any of the individual receiver branches. \|<br>\| Applicable for \| If SS-RSRP is used for L1-RSRP, RRC_CONNECTED intra-frequency, If SS-RSRP is being performed by wake-up receiver (WUR), RRC_IDLE for serving cell, RRC_INACTIVE for serving cell. Otherwise, RRC_IDLE intra-frequency, RRC_IDLE inter-frequency, RRC_INACTIVE intra-frequency, RRC_INACTIVE inter-frequency, RRC_CONNECTED intra-frequency, RRC_CONNECTED inter-frequency \|<br>5.1.2	CSI reference signal received power (CSI-RSRP)<br>\| Definition \| CSI reference signal received power (CSI-RSRP), is defined as the linear average over the power contributions (in [W]) of the resource elements of the antenna port(s) that carry CSI reference signals configured for RSRP measurements within the considered measurement frequency bandwidth in the configured CSI-RS occasions. For CSI-RSRP determination CSI reference signals transmitted on antenna port 3000 according to TS 38.211 [3] shall be used. If CSI-RSRP is used for L1-RSRP, CSI reference signals transmitted on antenna ports 3000, 3001 can be used for CSI-RSRP determination. For intra-frequency CSI-RSRP measurements, if the measurement gap is not configured, UE is not expected to measure the CSI-RS resource(s) outside of the active downlink bandwidth part. For frequency range 1, the reference point for the CSI-RSRP shall be the antenna connector of the UE. For frequency range 2, CSI-RSRP shall be measured based on the combined signal from antenna elements corresponding to a given receiver branch. For frequency range 1 and 2, if receiver diversity is in use by the UE, the reported CSI-RSRP value shall not be lower than the corresponding CSI-RSRP of any of the individual receiver branches. \|<br>\| Applicable for \| If CSI-RSRP is used for L1-RSRP, RRC_CONNECTED intra-frequency, Otherwise, RRC_CONNECTED intra-frequency, RRC_CONNECTED inter-frequency \|<br>Final CR in R1-2605091 is endorsed.<br>7-24 GHz | [Chair notes RAN1#125_eom](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_125/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23125_eom.docx)<br>[R1-2605008](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_125/Docs/R1-2605008.zip) |
| RAN1#126 | 10.5.3.1 Aspects of downlink-based CSI acquisition | CSI Acquisition / Prediction | Agreement | - On defining the CSI-RS pattern in M1 slots and M2 RBs, support both M1 and M2 can be larger than or equal to 1<br>  - For M1,<br>    - For the number of CSI-RS ports smaller than or equal to X, only M1=1 is supported.<br>    - For the number of CSI-RS ports larger than X, support M1 can be larger than 1<br>    - FFS value of X<br>  - FFS for M2 | [Chair notes RAN1#126_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126_eom1.docx)<br>`[126-R20-6GR-Aspects of DL based CSI acquisition]` |

| RAN1#126 | 10.5.3.1 Aspects of downlink-based CSI acquisition | CSI Acquisition / Prediction | Agreement | On the design of CDM group size and type for 6G CSI-RS, study and evaluate the following detailed candidates in addition to the ones supported in NR<br>- For Option 2-2: Larger CDM size<br>  - Candidate 2-2-1: cdm16-FD4-TD4<br>  - Candidate 2-2-2: cdm16-FD2-TD8<br>  - Candidate 2-2-3: cdm16-FD8-TD2<br>  - Candidate 2-2-4: cdm16-FD16<br>  - Candidate 2-2-5: cdm32-FD4-TD8<br>  - Candidate 2-2-6: cdm32-FD8-TD4<br>  - Candidate 2-2-7: cdm64-FD8-TD8<br>  - Candidate 2-2-8: cdm12y-FD12-TDy, where y = {1, 2, 4}<br>- For Option 2-3: New CDM type for NR CDM size<br>  - Candidate 2-3-1: cdm4-FD4<br>  - Candidate 2-3-2: cdm8-FD4-TD2<br>  - Candidate 2-3-3: cdm8-FD8<br>- Companies to report the applicable number of CSI-RS ports for each candidate in the simulation | [Chair notes RAN1#126_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126_eom1.docx)<br>`[126-R20-6GR-Aspects of DL based CSI acquisition]` |
| RAN1#126 | 10.5.3.1 Aspects of downlink-based CSI acquisition | CSI Acquisition / Prediction | Agreement | For CSI-RS sequence in 6G, take the NR PN sequence and mapping as the baseline for evaluation, further study the following aspects<br>- How to achieve low-PAPR for pre-coded CSI-RS by sequence and/or mapping, compared to NR PN sequence and its mapping<br>- Handle the case with high inter-cell interference /how to handle the case with high inter-cell interference<br>- CSI-RS sharing for different frequency density<br>- On sequence initialization, take NR UE specific configuration for initialization as the baseline, study whether other options are needed | [Chair notes RAN1#126_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126_eom1.docx)<br>`[126-R20-6GR-Aspects of DL based CSI acquisition]` |
| RAN1#126 | 10.5.3.1 Aspects of downlink-based CSI acquisition | CSI Acquisition / Prediction | Agreement | For 6G CSI-RS, study the following options to address the potential PAPR issue caused by precoded CSI-RS<br>- Option 1: BS implementation-based approach, e.g., use UE-transparent CDM-specific small cyclic shifts, or use small CDM group size<br>- Option 2: Map the PN sequence to the global subcarrier indices in the whole bandwidth sequentially<br>- Option 3: Multiply the PN sequence with an additional CDM group-specific scrambling sequence<br>- Option 4: Multiply the PN sequence with an additional port-specific scrambling sequence<br>- Option 5: Apply segmented phase rotation on the PN sequence | [Chair notes RAN1#126_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126_eom1.docx)<br>`[126-R20-6GR-Aspects of DL based CSI acquisition]` |
| RAN1#126 | 10.5.3.1 Aspects of downlink-based CSI acquisition | CSI Acquisition / Prediction | Agreement | For CSI-RS sharing Use case 2: Sharing a common set or subset of REs among CSI-RS with different frequency densities, study the following alternatives for discussing frequency density common sequence mapping<br>- Alt 1: Generate sequence for higher frequency density first, from which extract lower frequency density sequence<br>- Alt 2: Generate sequences for lower frequency density first, which are concatenated to form higher frequency density sequence | [Chair notes RAN1#126_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126_eom1.docx)<br>`[126-R20-6GR-Aspects of DL based CSI acquisition]` |
| RAN1#126 | 10.5.3.1 Aspects of downlink-based CSI acquisition | CSI Acquisition / Prediction | Agreement | - For CSI-RS sharing Use case 1: Sharing a common set or subset of REs among CSI-RS with different number of ports, taking Level 1 (Sharing granularity is CDM group level) as baseline,<br>- Study Level 2 (Sharing granularity can be smaller than a CDM group), with the following sub-levels<br>- Level 2-1: CDM group is decoded without assuming the knowledge of<br>- Level 2-2: CDM group is decoded assuming the knowledge of | [Chair notes RAN1#126_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126_eom1.docx)<br>`[126-R20-6GR-Aspects of DL based CSI acquisition]` |
| RAN1#126 | 10.5.3.1 Aspects of downlink-based CSI acquisition | CSI Acquisition / Prediction | Agreement | For 6GR CSI-RS, study CSI-RS design to achieve low CSI-RS overhead considering the following possible directions<br>- Direction 1: Sparse CSI-RS in frequency/spatial domain<br>  - Consider AI and/or non-AI prediction to reconstruct the channel at UE and/or NW side<br>  - Study sparse pattern<br>- Direction 2: Use non-orthogonal CSI-RS ports<br>  - Study the use of cyclic shifts or scrambling sequences across CSI-RS RBs, or non-orthogonal cover code<br>  - Inter CSI-RS port interference leakage is to be considered in the evaluations<br>- Note: Combinations of the directions can be considered.<br>- Note: At least total CSI-RS overhead, performance and UE/NW complexity are to be studied<br>- Note: Companies to report the assumed CSI-RS time-domain behavior including CSI-RS periodicity in the evaluations<br>- Note: companies to report how CSI-RS overhead can be reduced from system perspective | [Chair notes RAN1#126_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126_eom1.docx)<br>`[126-R20-6GR-Aspects of DL based CSI acquisition]` |
| RAN1#126 | 10.5.3.1 Aspects of downlink-based CSI acquisition | CSI Acquisition / Prediction | Agreement | For the study of explicit CSI feedback, the evaluation considers the following aspects:<br>- Form of explicit CSI<br>  - Opt-A: information facilitating NW reconstruction of the channel matrix H (e.g., NRX x NTX) per frequency granularity unit<br>    - Note:  NRX does not necessarily mean the number of UE Rx physical antenna(s)<br>  - Opt-B: information facilitating NW reconstruction of H’H per frequency granularity unit<br>  - Companies to report compression/quantization scheme used for the evaluation<br>- Handling of link adaptation via at least one of the following options<br>  - Opt-1: CQI/RI information in another report (e.g., based on CSI-RS or DMRS)<br>  - Opt-2: Assistance information (e.g., L1-SINR, L1-RSRP, Interference-plus-noise (IpN) information) in the same explicit CSI report or another report<br>  - Opt-3: the explicit feedback considering pre-whitening channel or channel covariance<br>  - Note: other options are not precluded. | [Chair notes RAN1#126_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126_eom1.docx)<br>`[126-R20-6GR-Aspects of DL based CSI acquisition]` |
| RAN1#126 | 10.5.3.1 Aspects of downlink-based CSI acquisition | CSI Acquisition / Prediction | Agreement | For evaluation on precoded CSI-RS without considering CSI-RS overhead reduction:<br>- Assume cell-specific wideband precoded CSI-RS, e.g., the number of precoded CSI-RS ports is same as the number of TXRUs at aNB<br>- Company reports the precoder of CSI-RS, and information/how to determinate the precoder.<br>- The following CSI feedback scheme can be considered for the evaluation:<br>- Option 1: Type I-like (W1W2) and/or eType II-like (W1W2Wf) CSI reporting with port-selection (PS) basis<br>- Option 2: Type I-like (W1W2) and/or eType II-like (W1W2Wf) CSI reporting with NR DFT basis with reconstruction information<br>- Option 3: Type I-like (W1W2) and/or eType II-like(W1W2Wf) CSI reporting with NR DFT basis (CSI-RS port precoder is transparent to UE)<br>- Company report how NW utilized the reported CSI for PDSCH precoder determination.<br>- FFS on other basis, e.g., the basis constructed from combined 2D DFT beams/ port-selection (PS) basis<br>- Benchmark:<br>  - Type I and eType II codebook with non-precoded CSI-RS<br>Companies report the QCL related assumptions<br>Note: Precoded CSI-RS with less density in spatial domain is to be discussed separately.<br>Note: downloadable basis/precoder (e.g. FD basis/precoder associated with each precoded CSI-RS port) can be considered additionally with precoded CSI-RS and the assumption/consideration for downloadable basis is taken into account<br>Note: the PAPR issue of precoded CSI-RS is to be studied. | [Chair notes RAN1#126_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126_eom1.docx)<br>`[126-R20-6GR-Aspects of DL based CSI acquisition]` |
| RAN1#126 | 10.5.3.1 Aspects of downlink-based CSI acquisition | CSI Acquisition / Prediction | Agreement | For the evaluation on PDSCH DMRS-based CSI for PDSCH link adaptation, the following is consideration:<br>- For channel measurement, the DMRS port(s) associated with data is considered as the baseline<br>  - FFS on whether to consider DMRS port(s) allocated with PDSCH but not associated with the data<br>- For interference measurement (when needed), company to report how to acquire interference information.<br>- Company to report the UE assumptions for DMRS-based CSI calculation.<br>- Email discussion for the templates and collect results for the use cases, e.g., fixed codebook, AI-CSI, CPI, downloadable/configurable codebook/basis, DMRS-based CSI, precoded CSI-RS. | [Chair notes RAN1#126_eom1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/tsg_ran/WG1_RL1/TSGR1_126/Inbox/Chair_notes/Chair%20notes%20RAN1%23126_eom1.docx)<br>`[126-R20-6GR-Aspects of DL based CSI acquisition]` |
